Every word of law in this repository comes from these sources. Lexifina reads them and records each act's dated versions, and the laws that made each change, in one corpus file. This repository is built from that file.

## What is in it

| Act | Title | Versions | Span |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| `syntagma` | Σύνταγμα της Ελλάδας | 5 | 2001-04-17 to 2019-12-24 |
| `nomos-4624-2019` | Αρχή Προστασίας Δεδομένων Προσωπικού Χαρακτήρα, μέτρα εφαρμογής του Κανονισμού (ΕΕ) 2016/679 του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου της 27ης Απριλίου 2016 για την προστασία των φυσικών προσώπων έναντι της επεξεργασίας δεδομένων προσωπικού χαρακτήρα και ενσωμάτωση στην εθνική νομοθεσία της Οδηγίας (ΕΕ) 2016/680 του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου της 27ης Απριλίου 2016 και άλλες διατάξεις. | 7 | 2019-08-29 to 2025-07-28 |
| `nomos-5160-2024` | Ενσωμάτωση της Οδηγίας (ΕΕ) 2022/2555 του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου, της 14ης Δεκεμβρίου 2022, σχετικά με μέτρα για υψηλό κοινό επίπεδο κυβερνοασφάλειας σε ολόκληρη την Ένωση, την τροποποίηση του Κανονισμού (ΕΕ) 910/2014 και της Οδηγίας (ΕΕ) 2018/1972, και την κατάργηση της Οδηγίας (ΕΕ) 2016/1148 (Οδηγία NIS 2) και άλλες διατάξεις. | 2 | 2024-11-27 to 2025-12-08 |
| `nomos-3471-2006` | Προστασία δεδομένων προσωπικού χαρακτήρα και της ιδιωτικής ζωής στον τομέα των ηλεκτρονικών επικοινωνιών και τροποποίηση του ν. 2472/1997. | 5 | 2006-06-28 to 2012-04-10 |
| `nomos-5178-2025` | Μέτρα για την ισόρροπη εκπροσώπηση των φύλων σε θέσεις διευθυντικών στελεχών των εισηγμένων εταιρειών, των μη εισηγμένων ανωνύμων εταιρειών και των δημοσίων επιχειρήσεων – Ενσωμάτωση της Οδηγίας (Ε.Ε.) 2022/2381 του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου της 23ης Νοεμβρίου 2022 – Ρυθμίσεις για την ενδυνάμωση των πιλοτικών προγραμμάτων για την ενίσχυση της κοινωνικής συνοχής – Πρόγραμμα Δημογραφικής Ανάπτυξης και άλλες διατάξεις. | 3 | 2025-02-14 to 2026-07-24 |
| `nomos-5188-2025` | Μέτρα εφαρμογής του Κανονισμού (ΕΕ) 2022/868 (πράξη για τη διακυβέρνηση δεδομένων) - Ορισμός αρμόδιας αρχής για την εφαρμογή του Κανονισμού (ΕΕ) 2024/903 (Κανονισμός για τη διαλειτουργική Ευρώπη) - Ηλεκτρονική εφαρμογή «mystreet» - Ρυθμίσεις για την προώθηση του ψηφιακού μετασχηματισμού και λοιπές διατάξεις. | 4 | 2025-03-28 to 2026-07-20 |
| `nomos-5202-2025` | Λήψη μέτρων εφαρμογής του Κανονισμού (ΕΕ) 2019/452 για τη θέσπιση πλαισίου ελέγχου άμεσων ξένων επενδύσεων στην Ένωση για λόγους ασφάλειας ή δημόσιας τάξης. | 1 | 2025-05-23 to 2025-05-23 |
| `nomos-5232-2025` | Καθορισμός αδικημάτων και κυρώσεων σε βάρος φυσικών και νομικών προσώπων για παραβίαση των περιοριστικών μέτρων της Ευρωπαϊκής Ένωσης, ενσωμάτωση Οδηγίας (ΕΕ) 2024/1226 του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου της 24ης Απριλίου 2024 σχετικά με τον ορισμό των ποινικών αδικημάτων και των κυρώσεων για την παραβίαση των περιοριστικών μέτρων της Ένωσης και την τροποποίηση της Οδηγίας (ΕΕ) 2018/1673 και λοιπές διατάξεις. | 1 | 2025-09-22 to 2025-09-22 |
| `nomos-5214-2025` | Πλαίσιο για την ενίσχυση της ανθεκτικότητας: Διατάξεις για την πολιτική προστασία και το Πυροσβεστικό Σώμα. | 1 | 2025-06-27 to 2025-06-27 |
| `nomos-5220-2025` | Αναβάθμιση της ασφάλειας των σιδηροδρόμων, αξιολόγηση προσωπικού σιδηροδρόμων, ενίσχυση εποπτείας σιδηροδρομικού δικτύου, οργανωτική ενίσχυση της Ρυθμιστικής Αρχής Σιδηροδρόμων, του Εθνικού Οργανισμού Διερεύνησης Αεροπορικών και Σιδηροδρομικών Ατυχημάτων και Ασφάλειας Μεταφορών και της εταιρείας «Σιδηρόδρομοι Ελλάδος Μονοπρόσωπη Α.Ε.» και άλλες διατάξεις. | 1 | 2025-07-21 to 2025-07-21 |
| `nomos-5255-2025` | Σύσταση και λειτουργία Ανεξάρτητης Αρχής Ελέγχου της Αγοράς και Προστασίας του Καταναλωτή, ρυθμίσεις για την Επιτροπή Ανταγωνισμού και λοιπές διατάξεις. | 4 | 2025-11-28 to 2026-07-10 |
| `nomos-5261-2025` | Ρυθμίσεις για τη δέσμευση, χρήση, μεταφορά και αποθήκευση διοξειδίου του άνθρακα – Ενσωμάτωση της Οδηγίας 2009/31/ΕΚ του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου της 23ης Απριλίου 2009 σχετικά με την αποθήκευση διοξειδίου του άνθρακα σε γεωλογικούς σχηματισμούς και για την τροποποίηση της οδηγίας 85/337/ΕΟΚ του Συμβουλίου, των οδηγιών του Ευρωπαϊκού Κοινοβουλίου και του Συμβουλίου 2000/60/ΕΚ, 2001/80/ΕΚ, 2004/35/ΕΚ, 2006/12/ΕΚ και 2008/1/ ΕΚ και του κανονισμού (ΕΚ) 1013/2006 (L 140). | 2 | 2025-12-12 to 2026-05-05 |
| `nomos-5176-2025` | Ρυθμίσεις για την ενίσχυση του ερασιτεχνικού και του επαγγελματικού αθλητισμού και άλλες διατάξεις. | 2 | 2025-02-07 to 2026-07-30 |
| `nomos-5226-2025` | Αναμόρφωση πλαισίου και διαδικασιών επιστροφών πολιτών τρίτων χωρών – Λοιπές ρυθμίσεις του Υπουργείου Μετανάστευσης και Ασύλου. | 1 | 2025-09-08 to 2025-09-08 |
| `nomos-5229-2025` | Κοινωνική αντιπαροχή, κοινωνική μίσθωση, τριτεκνική ιδιότητα και άλλες διατάξεις. | 2 | 2025-09-12 to 2026-06-25 |
| `nomos-5237-2025` | Σύσταση Ακαδημιών Επαγγελματικής Κατάρτισης – Ένταξη των Ακαδημιών στο Εθνικό Σύστημα Επαγγελματικής Εκπαίδευσης και Κατάρτισης, άλλα θέματα επαγγελματικής κατάρτισης και λοιπές διατάξεις. | 1 | 2025-10-13 to 2025-10-13 |
| `nomos-5258-2025` | Εποπτική Μονάδα Ασφάλειας Λιμένων και Πλοίων και άλλες διατάξεις. | 1 | 2025-12-09 to 2025-12-09 |
| `nomos-5263-2025` | Ίδρυση Ελληνικού Εργοστασίου Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης – Συνεποπτεία της Η.Δ.Υ.Κ.Α. Μ.Α.Ε. από τα Υπουργεία Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Υγείας – Λοιπές διατάξεις. | 3 | 2025-12-19 to 2026-07-20 |

## Dates

Each commit is dated at noon (Europe/Athens) on the day the change took effect. The other dates, enacted and published, are trailers.

When a law gives no date of its own, the default rule applies: Each revision resolution of the Constitution states when it takes effect: "Οι αναθεωρούμενες διατάξεις, εφόσον δεν ορίζεται διαφορετικά σε κάποια από αυτές, τίθενται σε ισχύ με τη δημοσίευση του Ψηφίσματος αυτού στην Εφημερίδα της Κυβερνήσεως" (clause Δ of ΦΕΚ Α΄ 84/2001, Α΄ 102/2008 and Α΄ 187/2019). Every date in this corpus is that publication day; no general rule is applied. https://ia37rg02wpsa01.blob.core.windows.net/fek/01/2019/20190100187.pdf

## How the history is built

`tools/build.py` turns the corpus into this history in one pass: one commit per law, on the day it took effect. The same corpus always gives the same commit hashes. `tools/check_repo.py` reads every version back from git and compares it with the corpus. `tools/on.py` shows the law as it stood on any date. The corpus format is described in `tools/SCHEMA.md`.

- Corpus: sha256 `7d37120d5b6fe3da6b3b52ef19839cd6a6c68018f73ca29f2318e7d08c810bcc` (16467289 bytes).

- Python 3.12.10; git version 2.46.0.windows.1.

No second build of this corpus has been compared yet.

## Decisions made by hand

- Greece's Constitution is built from the Government Gazette alone: no official consolidated text exists, and each revision resolution ordered the whole text republished, so the history is the three whole-text republications that have a text layer (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 85/2001, 120/2008, 211/2019) and the two revision resolutions between them (Α΄ 102/2008, 187/2019).
- The 1975 text (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 111/1975) and the 1986 issue fetched (Α΄ 23/1986) are page-image scans with no text layer; by the no-OCR rule they are not read, so the history starts with the 2001 text, credited to the publisher as the oldest version available. Their issue numbers could not be checked against the scans; the 1986 demotic text is cited as ΦΕΚ Α΄ 24/14.3.1986 by the 2001, 2008 and 2019 resolutions.
- Each revision is two versions: on the day its resolution was published, the text before it with only the revised articles changed, a revised paragraph in the resolution's own quoted words (clause Γ: they are the only authentic text of revised provisions); then, on the republication day, the whole republished text, its own differences credited to the republication issue (kind publisher). The build stops if a changed paragraph in a revised article matches no quotation of the resolution (similarity under 0.9).
- The articles each resolution revises are read from its part A: 2008: arts. 57, 79, 101, 115; 2019: arts. 21, 32, 54, 62, 68, 73, 86, 96, 101Α, 112, 113, 114, 115, 119. The diffs change exactly these articles, and the publisher's asterisks in the republications (*** for 2008, **** for 2019) mark the same articles, except 115 (2008: paragraphs deleted and renumbered; 2019: repealed whole).
- The 2019 resolution's own text of art. 32(4) reads "εκλέγειται" (a misprint); the revision day keeps it and the 2019 republication's "εκλέγεται" is credited to the republication.
- The 2019 resolution says the whole 2008 text was published in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 102/2.6.2008; that issue holds only the resolution, and the whole text is ΦΕΚ Α΄ 120/27.6.2008 (its masthead and its order cite the 2008 resolution).
- Repealed provisions read as the 2019 republication marks them, e.g. "(Το άρθρο 113 καταργείται.)", from the revision day: the note states only the repeal.
- One file per article (art-NNN; 5Α is art-005-a), one paragraph or list item per line, starting with the line "Άρθρο N" as printed. The part, section and chapter headings that open an article are the lines before it in that article's file, so the files read in order give the whole text. Interpretive declarations ("Ερμηνευτική δήλωση") stay in their article. The preamble is prooimio.md.
- Text normalisation, done once when the text is parsed: the 2001 issue's legacy font holds Windows-1253 bytes (its byte 0x88 is the ano teleia); Latin look-alike letters set inside Greek words (A, o, ...) become Greek; numeral signs and apostrophes become ’; straight double quotes become «»; spaced dashes become –; words broken at a line end are joined; the publisher's asterisks are removed.
- Paragraph breaks come from the PDF line geometry: a paragraph starts at an indented line. Lines spanning both columns (mastheads, the President of Parliament's order, footnotes), running headers, page numbers and lines smaller than the body type are not text.
- The newest version is the current official text: it is the whole-text republication ΦΕΚ Α΄ 211/24.12.2019 itself, and the Hellenic Parliament's constitutional-history page, captured 4 October 2026, lists the 2019 revision as the latest (the fourth revision of the 1975 Constitution).
- Phase 1 scope: the Constitution first. ν. 4624/2019 (personal data) and the Penal Code need every amending law replayed from the Gazette; see PROGRESS.md.

- ν. 4624/2019 is built from the Government Gazette alone: the act as published (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 137/2019) and, for each amendment, the amending law's own issue. Greece publishes no official consolidated text.
- legalize-gr was to be the text source, but its file for ΦΕΚ Α΄ 137/2019 agrees word for word with the issue in only 53 of 87 articles: 4 are missing (art-013, art-036, art-050, art-081) and 30 lose or interleave text across the two columns. So every word is read from the ΦΕΚ PDFs with this folder's column reader, and legalize-gr is used only to find amending laws.
- Amending laws were found three ways: (1) a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue 2019-2026 in legalize-gr for any article that names a provision of ν. 4624/2019 outside a quotation and carries an amending verb or heading; (2) the official search.et.gr relation index, as copied into legalize-gr's `amends` field; (3) the amendment notes of the Data Protection Authority's consolidated text. (1) finds ν. 4829/2021, 5002/2022, 5043/2023, 5197/2025 and 5221/2025 (plus Α΄ 77/2023, which only cites the law); (2) lists 5002/2022, 5197/2025 and 5221/2025 (its other rows, Α΄ 77/2023, 223/2025 and 9/2026, only cite ν. 4624/2019); (3) names 4829/2021, 5002/2022, 5043/2023 and 5221/2025. ν. 5043/2023 was first found through (3): it repeals in a list item, which the first version of the scan missed. Every one of the five was then read in its own issue.
- Article 86 of ν. 4624/2019 is dated from 2019-09-01 by its own par. 6; the rest of the act from its publication, 2019-08-29 (art. 87).
- Every other version is dated by the amending law's own commencement clause, read from its issue (see the parse log); each says publication in the Gazette for the provisions used here. The publication day is the date printed on the issue's masthead. Α΄ 228/2022 carries a digital signature of 10.12.2022 00:27 and Α΄ 76/2025 one of 19.05.2025; the masthead dates (9.12.2022 and 16.5.2025) are used.
- Units are articles (art-NNN, art-NNN-a for Α). An article's text is its heading lines (chapter and section headings that open it), "Άρθρο N", its title on one line, then one paragraph or lettered item per line. Section numerals, which the Gazette sets with Greek Ι beside Latin V, are written in Latin.
- A repealed paragraph or item is removed (art. 18 par. 6 in 2021, art. 44 περ. ιζ in 2022, art. 11 par. 6 in 2023). From 2025 art. 18 par. 6 reads «6. [Καταργείται].» because ν. 5221/2025 restates the article that way.
- ν. 5002/2022 art. 38 adds art. 45Α "after art. 45": it is placed before the Τμήμα ΙΙ heading, which opens art. 46, as the Data Protection Authority's text also places it.
- ν. 5002/2022 art. 42 misprints the law as "ν. 4264/2019 (Α’ 137)"; its heading and the issue number name ν. 4624/2019, so it is applied.
- Where an amending provision describes a change and then restates the result, the restated text is used, and the parse checks the description against the old text; the small wording changes the restatement makes on its own are listed below.
- Check against the Data Protection Authority's consolidated text (PDF modified 2025-12-10): the latest version, 2025-07-28, agrees with it word for word in every article and heading but two. Art. 84Α: the Authority's text lacks the third sentence of par. 7 added by ν. 5197/2025 art. 56 (read in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 76/2025). Art. 18 par. 8: the Authority's text still cites «π.δ. 50/2001», which ν. 5221/2025 art. 154 restates as «π.δ. 85/2022 (Α’ 39)». The 2023-04-13 version (before both) differs from it only in art-018. Comparison is by words, ignoring punctuation, quote marks, accents, case and spacing.

- ν. 5160/2024 is built from the Government Gazette alone: the act as published (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 195/2024) and the one law that amends it, read in its own issue (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 223/2025). Greece publishes no official consolidated text.
- Amending laws were looked for three ways: (1) a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from 2024 to Α΄ 177/2026 in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd) for an article naming a provision of ν. 5160/2024 outside a quotation with an amending verb or heading: it finds only Α΄ 223/2025; (2) legalize-gr's `amended_by` field for Α΄ 195/2024, copied from the official search.et.gr index: Α΄ 223/2025 only; (3) the official index's timeline for the issue: Α΄ 223/2025 (Τροποποίηση) and Α΄ 175/2025 (Ακύρωση). Α΄ 175/2025 (ν. 5236/2025, critical entities) was read in full: it cites ν. 5160/2024 but amends none of its provisions.
- Annexes I and II of ν. 5160/2024 (the sectors of high criticality and the other critical sectors) are printed in the issue in a font whose text layer maps to no characters, so they cannot be read without OCR and are left out; the articles that refer to them are complete.
- Units are articles (art-NNN). An article's text is the chapter and part headings that open it, "Άρθρο N", its title on one line, then one paragraph or lettered item per line, as in the ν. 4624/2019 part.
- Each version is dated by the law's own commencement clause (see the parse log): publication, the date on the issue's masthead. Art. 3 par. 2 περ. στ) υποπερ. στβ) of ν. 5160/2024 applies one year after publication (art. 45 par. 2); its words are part of the act from publication, so it is shown from then.
- Where ν. 5256/2025 describes a word change and then restates the paragraph, the restated text is used and the parse checks that the described change applied to the old paragraph gives it; small wording differences the restatement makes on its own are listed below.

- Amending laws were looked for in three ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue 2006-2026 in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd) for an article naming a provision of the law outside a quotation with an amending verb or heading; legalize-gr's `amended_by` field (copied from the official search.et.gr index), which lists Α΄ 34/2015 and 22/2011; and the official index's timeline for the issue, which also lists Α΄ 136/2009, 152/2007, 184/2020, 195/2024, 202/2024 and 175/2025. - Art. 31 of ν. 3471/2006 puts the law in force on publication (28.6.2006) except Chapter One, one month later: arts. 1-17 are added on 28.7.2006. Arts. 18-30 amend ν. 2472/1997 and are kept as enacted.

- Restated wording: ν. 5317/2026 art. 117 par. 1: the restatement reads «» where the described change gives «και». ν. 5317/2026 art. 117 par. 1 restates art. 9 par. 1 «μετά από νομοτεχνικές βελτιώσεις» (after drafting improvements), in its own words: its items now open with a capital and end with a full stop, so the «και» before item ζ) goes. The restatement is the amending law’s own text and governs.
- Restated wording: ν. 5317/2026 art. 117 par. 4: the restatement reads «στη» where the described change gives «στο». In art. 23 par. 3 the instruction adds «που είναι εγγεγραμμένοι στο Μητρώο Ελεγκτών», while the restatement prints «εγγεγραμμένοι στη Μητρώο Ελεγκτών» (so in the Gazette text layer too). The restatement is the amending law’s own text, so it governs and is kept as printed; «στη» there is evidently a misprint for «στο».
- ν. 5261/2025 (carbon dioxide capture, transport and storage) is given as published in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 231/2025, then with each later amendment, read in the amending law's own Gazette issue: 67/2026 (ν. 5299/2026).
- Amending laws were looked for three ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from 2025 to Α΄ 177/2026, in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd) where it has the issue and in the Gazette PDF where it does not (2026: 58-127, 143, 144, 147, 148, 174, 175), every mention read by hand; and the official search.et.gr relation index for the issue (as read on 2026-10-30), which lists ΦΕΚ Α΄ 67/2026 only. Amending: ν. 5299/2026 arts. 68, 69 and 88 par. 2. Other mentions only refer to it: ν. 5299/2026 art. 50 (the subjects of its Part), the Spatial Planning and Urban Planning Code ratified by ν. 5306/2026 (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 88/2026), whose source tables cite art. 75 pars. 3, 4, 5, 8 and 9 as amendments of other laws and whose list of repealed provisions (its Annex Α΄) does not name ν. 5261/2025, ν. 5314/2026 art. 383 and ΦΕΚ Α΄ 122/2026 art. 34. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were not searched.
- ν. 5299/2026 art. 68 corrects errata («Διόρθωση παροραμάτων») by amendment, with no words on when the corrections apply, so they are dated by the law's commencement, its publication (5 May 2026), like its other changes.
- A repealed paragraph is removed (art. 75 par. 9); the paragraphs after it keep their numbers.
- In the restatements and the added article of ν. 5299/2026 (arts. 68-69, ΦΕΚ Α΄ 67/2026) the 2 of CO₂ is set as a smaller digit after «CO» (8 pt against 10.5 pt), which the text layer reads as a plain 2; the parse checks that every such 2 on those pages is in the smaller type and writes it CO₂, as ν. 5261/2025 itself mostly does. Its annexes keep the forms their own text layer gives (CO2, and in Annex IV CΟ2 with a Greek Ο); the check of described changes reads these forms as one word, and a restated line that differs from the published line only in this form or in line breaks keeps the published line (listed under Layout), so each change shows only the amending law's changes. Not a change of wording.
- Where an amending article lists its changes and then restates the article, the restatement (the amending law's own text) is used, and the parse checks that the listed changes applied to the old text give it; words the instruction adds or replaces without quoting them are taken from the restatement at the place it names. Any other difference is listed below with the decision that accepts it.

- ν. 5226/2025 (returns of third-country nationals) is given as published in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 154/2025, its only version: no later law amends it. Amending laws were looked for in two ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from the law's year to Α΄ 177/2026 in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd), for an article naming a provision of the law outside a quotation with an amending verb or heading, and the official search.et.gr relation index for the issue (legalize-gr's `amended_by` field, which lists no Α΄ issue). Neither finds any. legalize-gr lacks the ΦΕΚ Α΄ issues 58-127, 143, 144, 147, 148, 174 and 175 of 2026; those were read in the Gazette PDFs themselves and every mention of the law in them checked by hand: none amends it. The timeline of the official index for the issue, fetched on 2026-11-01, lists no Α΄ issue as amending it. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were not searched.

- ν. 5023/2023 (equal treatment regardless of disability or chronic illness and other provisions) is given as published in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 34/2023, its only version: no later law amends it. Amending laws were looked for in three ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from 2023 to Α΄ 177/2026 in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd), for an article naming a provision of the law outside a quotation with an amending verb or heading; the ΦΕΚ Α΄ issues of 2026 that legalize-gr lacks (58-127, 143, 144, 147, 148, 174 and 175), read in the Gazette PDFs themselves; and the timeline of the official search.et.gr index for the issue, fetched on 2026-11-02, which lists no Α΄ issue against it. The scan finds only Α΄ 121/2025, a presidential decree codifying the law on equal treatment, which names art. 3 of ν. 5023/2023 (an amendment of ν. 4443/2016) only in its tables of sources and correspondence. The 2026 issues read in the PDFs do not mention it. None amends ν. 5023/2023. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were searched only through the official index.
- ν. 5023/2023 is dated 2023-02-17, the date on the issue's masthead: its commencement article (art-027) reads «Η ισχύς του παρόντος νόμου αρχίζει από τη δημοσίευσή του στην Εφημερίδα της Κυβερνήσεως.». Provisions it starts later are shown from publication.
- Units are articles (art-NNN), as in the other Greek parts: the headings that open an article, "Άρθρο N", its title, then one paragraph or item per line. Annexes and signatures are not units.

- ν. 5025/2023 is dated 2023-02-24, the date on the issue's masthead: its commencement article (art-028) reads «1. Με την επιφύλαξη της παρ. 2, η ισχύς του παρόντος αρχίζει από τη δημοσίευσή του στην Εφημερίδα της Κυβερνήσεως. 3. Η ισχύς της περ. β’ της παρ. 3 του άρθρου 24 του ν. 4373/2016 (Α’ 49), ως προς την κύρωση της αυτοδίκαιης έκπτωσης των μελών του Διοικητικού Συμβουλίου αθλητικού φορέα, το οποίο δεν εισάγει ή δεν επικαιροποιεί στο ηλεκτρονικό μητρώο αθλητικών φορέων τα απαιτούμενα στοιχεία εντός των οριζόμενων προθεσμιών, αρχίζει από την 1η.1.2024.». Provisions it starts later are shown from publication.

- ν. 5313/2026 art. 123 par. 4 repeals art. 45 "from the date the revocation decision of par. 3 is issued", par. 3 naming the decision that revokes the inclusion decision ΤΕΑ/10/3.018-κε’/12.9.2024 of the action with ΟΠΣ code «6017450». That decision is a ministerial act outside the Gazette. It is identified by its record in the official Diavgeia transparency programme: ΑΔΑ 9ΠΕΔ46ΜΤΛΒ-47Ψ, protocol number ΤΕΑ/10/3.018-σλη΄ (the same series as the inclusion decision), issuer ΥΠΟΥΡΓΕΙΟ ΠΡΟΣΤΑΣΙΑΣ ΤΟΥ ΠΟΛΙΤΗ, subject «ΑΠΟΦΑΣΗ ΑΝΑΚΛΗΣΗΣ ΑΠΟΦΑΣΗΣ ΕΝΤΑΞΗΣ ΠΡΑΞΗΣ», date of issue 2026-08-17; it is the only revocation decision the programme lists for that code after ν. 5313/2026 was published. The repeal is dated by that day; the date comes from the record, not from the decision's own text. Par. 4 β) (a joint ministerial decision, Β΄ 5629) is outside this act.

It names no words of art. 15 and removes none: it narrows what the article covers from 2024. Art. 15 is therefore kept as published, and this partial repeal is recorded here rather than as a version.
- A repealed article is removed from the day the repeal takes effect, as elsewhere in the corpus. Arts. 16 and 45 amended other laws (ν. 4662/2020 and ν. 3852/2010); their repeal is applied to the words of ν. 4937/2022 here, and what it does to those other laws is not part of this record.
- Where an amending article lists its changes and then restates the article, the restatement (the amending law's own text) is used, and the parse checks that the listed changes applied to the old text give it; words the instruction adds or replaces without quoting them are taken from the restatement at the place it names. Any other difference is listed below with the decision that accepts it.

- ν. 4955/2022 starts in steps, by its own commencement article (art-068): «1. Η ισχύς του παρόντος αρχίζει από τη δημοσίευσή του στην Εφημερίδα της Κυβερνήσεως με την επιφύλαξη των παρ. 2 και 3. 2. Η ισχύς των άρθρων 1 έως 25 του Μέρους Α’ αρχίζει από τις 13 Φεβρουαρίου 2023 πλην της περ. β’ της παρ. 2 του εσωτερικού άρθρου 64 του ν. 2960/2001 (Α’ 265) του άρθρου 13 του παρόντος και της παρ. 3 του εσωτερικού άρθρου 65 του ίδιου νόμου του άρθρου 14 του παρόντος που ισχύουν από τη δημοσίευση του παρόντος. 3. Η ισχύς του άρθρου 52 αρχίζει από τις 5 Ιουλίου 2022.» So the first version (2022-07-14, publication, the date on the issue's masthead) holds arts. 26-68 and, of Part Α, only the two sub-provisions in force on publication; Part Α (arts. 1-25) is added in full on 2023-02-13, credited to the law itself, the day art. 68 par. 2 names.
- ν. 4955/2022 arts. 13 and 14 restate arts. 64 and 65 of ν. 2960/2001 (the National Customs Code). Art. 68 par. 2 starts on publication item β) of par. 2 of the restated art. 64 (art. 13) and par. 3 of the restated art. 65 (art. 14). In the first version those two articles hold only the lines that carry them, as printed: the article's heading and title, its instruction (which lists every change it makes to the inner article), the inner article's heading and title, and the sub-provision itself, for art. 13 after the opening words of its par. 2, which item β) completes. The rest of each article is added on 2023-02-13. The instruction and the inner headings are shown from publication because they are the words that carry those sub-provisions into ν. 2960/2001.
- ν. 4955/2022 art. 52 (a new second sentence of art. 146 par. 3 of ν. 4808/2021) starts on 5 July 2022 by art. 68 par. 3, nine days before the law was published (14 July 2022). As for every law in this corpus, the text is shown from publication; the earlier day the law gives it, 2022-07-05, is recorded here and not as a version.

- An Act of Legislative Content (Πράξη Νομοθετικού Περιεχομένου, Constitution art. 44 par. 1) has the force of law from its publication and is then ratified by a law. The Act of 22.8.2020 is credited as itself (kind "decree"), dated by its publication in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 161/2020 (22 August 2020), from which its art. δέκατο τρίτο puts it in force. ν. 4722/2020 art. 2 (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 177/2020, 15 September 2020) ratifies it and reprints its text; it changes nothing in art. πέμπτο, so the ratification is recorded here and not as a version.

Amending laws were looked for in three ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from 2020 to Α΄ 177/2026 in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd), for an article naming a provision of the law outside a quotation with an amending verb or heading; the ΦΕΚ Α΄ issues of 2026 that legalize-gr lacks (58-127, 143, 144, 147, 148, 174 and 175), read in the Gazette PDFs themselves, every mention read by hand; and the timeline of the official search.et.gr index for the issue, fetched on 2026-11-02, whose only later Α΄ row is ΦΕΚ Α΄ 200/2020 (its Β΄ rows are ministerial decisions, which do not amend a statute). The scan finds mentions in thirteen issues, each read by hand: ν. 4736/2020 art. 27 (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 200/2020, the issue the index lists) declares the true meaning of art. 4 item ιη) of ν. 4557/2018 as amended by art. 3 of ν. 4734/2020, without changing the words of either; and ΦΕΚ Α΄ 256/2020, 68/2021, 124/2021, 215/2021, 23/2022, 94/2022, 150/2022, 203/2022, 25/2023, 41/2023, 137/2023 and 164/2023 each grant further sums, or extend the time for commitments, for the defence credits approved by arts. 37 and 38 of ν. 4734/2020, by adding to the laws those articles amended (ν. 4361/2016 art. 32 and ν. 4258/2014 art. 26); the words of ν. 4734/2020 itself stay as published. None amends ν. 4734/2020. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were searched only through the official index. Its commencement article (art. 39) starts par. 5 of art. 12 of ν. 4557/2018, as art. 7 amends it, on 10 July 2020, before the law was published (8 October 2020). As for every law in this corpus, the text is shown from publication; that earlier day, 2020-07-10, is recorded here and not as a version.
- ν. 4734/2020 is dated 2020-10-08, the date on the issue's masthead: its commencement article (art-039) reads «Η ισχύς των διατάξεων του παρόντος αρχίζει από τη δημοσίευσή του στην Εφημερίδα της Κυβερνήσεως, εξαιρουμένης της παρ. 5 του άρθρου 12 του ν. 4557/2018 (Α’ 139), όπως τροποποιείται με το άρθρο 7 του παρόντος, η ισχύς της οποίας αρχίζει από τις 10.7.2020.». Provisions it starts later are shown from publication.
- Units are articles (art-NNN), as in the other Greek parts: the headings that open an article, "Άρθρο N", its title, then one paragraph or item per line. Annexes and signatures are not units.
- ν. 5053/2023 (strengthening work: transparent and predictable working conditions (Directive (EU) 2019/1152), simpler digital procedures and the digital work card, and the Ministry of Labour and the Labour Inspectorate) is given as published in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 158/2023, its only version: no later law amends it. The scan finds mentions in two issues, each read by hand. Presidential decree 62/2025 (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 121/2025), the Labour Code, codifies provisions of ν. 5053/2023 among its own articles and repeals only presidential decree 80/2022, so it changes no words of ν. 5053/2023. ν. 5239/2025 (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 178/2025, the issue the index lists) arts. 4, 5 and 20 amend arts. 73, 75, 190 and 578 of that Code, the articles "with which" arts. 6, 5, 10 and 21 of ν. 5053/2023 were codified: they change the Code's words, and the words of ν. 5053/2023 itself stay as published. None amends ν. 5053/2023. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were searched only through the official index.

- Amending laws were looked for three ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from 2024 to Α΄ 177/2026, in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd) where it has the issue and in the Gazette PDF where it does not (2026: 58-127, 143, 144, 147, 148, 174, 175), every mention of the law read by hand; and the official search.et.gr relation index for the issue, fetched on 2026-11-02, whose only later Α΄ row is ΦΕΚ Α΄ 158/2024 (its Β΄ row, ΦΕΚ Β΄ 4765/2024, read in the Gazette, is a Cadastre decision that cites art. 28 only). Amending: ν. 5142/2024 art. 5 (ΦΕΚ Α΄ 158/2024), which restates art. 29. ΦΕΚ Α΄ 56/2025 art. 29 names it, a reference only. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were searched only through the official index.
- ν. 5142/2024 art. 5 says it replaces «Σεπτεμβρίου» by «Οκτωβρίου» and adds a second sentence, then restates art. 29 "after legislative-drafting improvements"; the restated article has one sentence, the old one with the new month. The restatement (ν. 5142/2024's own text) governs and is used as printed. Its par. 2 applies the change also to declarations filed from 15 September 2024, before it was published (4 October 2024); as for every law in this corpus, the text is shown from publication, and that earlier day is recorded here and not as a version.
- Where an amending article lists its changes and then restates the article, the restatement (the amending law's own text) is used, and the parse checks that the listed changes applied to the old text give it; words the instruction adds or replaces without quoting them are taken from the restatement at the place it names. Any other difference is listed below with the decision that accepts it.
- ν. 5123/2024 starts in steps, by its own commencement article (art-031): «1. Η ισχύς των Μερών Α’ και Β’ αρχίζει από τη δημοσίευση της απόφασης της παρ. 1 του άρθρου 24, περί έναρξης της παραγωγικής λειτουργίας του Ηλεκτρονικού Μητρώου Ενεχύρων, άλλως την 31η Δεκεμβρίου 2024. 2. Η ισχύς του Μέρους Γ’ αρχίζει από τη δημοσίευση του παρόντος, εξαιρουμένου του άρθρου 27, η ισχύς του οποίου αρχίζει από τη 16η Σεπτεμβρίου 2024.» Art. 24 par. 1 leaves the Register's first day to a decision of the Board of the Hellenic Cadastre, and art. 24 par. 3 lets the Ministers of Justice and of Digital Governance move the start of Part Β. So the first version (2024-07-19, publication, the date on the issue's masthead) holds arts. 28-30 and art. 31 (the commencement article, shown from publication since it is the provision that sets those days); art. 27 follows on 2024-09-16, Part Α (arts. 1-2) on 2024-12-31 and Part Β (arts. 3-26) on 2025-06-30, each credited to the law itself.
- The start of Parts Α and Β was dated from the decisions themselves, read in the Gazette (series Β΄): joint ministerial decision 41802 ΕΞ 2024 (ΦΕΚ Β΄ 7024/2024, 20 December 2024), made under art. 24 par. 3 and art. 31, moves the start of Part Β to 30.6.2025; the Board's decision under art. 24 par. 1 (ΦΕΚ Β΄ 3333/2025, published 30 June 2025) fixes 30.06.2025, a Monday, as the day the Register starts operating. A search of the official search.et.gr index for the Register's name in series Β΄ (2024 and 2025) finds those two and three later Board decisions on how the Register works (ΦΕΚ Β΄ 2882/2025, 4613/2025, 6469/2025), none of which dates a Part. No decision under art. 24 par. 1 was published by 31 December 2024, so Part Α, which no decision moved, is dated by art. 31 par. 1's other day, 2024-12-31; Part Β is dated 2025-06-30, the day both decisions name.

- ν. 4654/2020 (the election of Members of Parliament (amendments to the electoral law, presidential decree 26/2012)) is given as published in ΦΕΚ Α΄ 15/2020, its only version: no later law amends it. Amending laws were looked for in three ways: a full-text scan of every ΦΕΚ Α΄ issue from 2020 to Α΄ 177/2026 in legalize-gr (commit 36cb88a374cd), for an article naming a provision of the law outside a quotation with an amending verb or heading; the ΦΕΚ Α΄ issues of 2026 that legalize-gr lacks (58-127, 143, 144, 147, 148, 174 and 175), read in the Gazette PDFs themselves, every mention read by hand; and the timeline of the official search.et.gr index for the issue, fetched on 2026-11-02, which lists no later issue acting on it. The scan finds no article that names it. None amends ν. 4654/2020. Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were searched only through the official index. Its commencement article (art. 3) starts it on publication "subject to art. 54 of the Constitution", whose par. 1 makes a law on the electoral system apply from the elections after next unless it says otherwise by a two-thirds majority. That is a condition on when it applies, not on its words: as for the Constitution here, it is recorded in the text and here, not in the dates.

Issues after Α΄ 177/2026 were searched only through the official index.
- ΦΕΚ Α΄ 118/2025 art. 57 par. 2 extends "from its expiry, for six more months" the time limit of the second sentence of art. 87. It names no words of art. 87 and changes none, so art. 87 is kept as published and the extension is recorded here rather than as a version.
- ν. 5326/2026 art. 67 adds the words «και του κατά περίπτωση συναρμόδιου Υπουργού» to the first sentence of art. 47 par. 1 without naming where; they are placed where the restatement has them, after «Με απόφαση του Υπουργού Δικαιοσύνης».
- Where an amending article lists its changes and then restates the article, the restatement (the amending law's own text) is used, and the parse checks that the listed changes applied to the old text give it; words the instruction adds or replaces without quoting them are taken from the restatement at the place it names. Any other difference is listed below with the decision that accepts it.

## What AI did and did not do

The scripts that read the sources and build this repository were written by Claude Code running Claude Opus 5.5 (`claude-opus-5-5`) under Lexifina's direction.

No model wrote, translated or corrected legal text. No model runs at build time. Every word of law in this repository comes from the official sources above.

The commit messages are written by `build.py` from the source data.

Model use at data-build time:
- Claude Code with Claude Opus 5.5 (claude-opus-5-5) wrote Lexifina's code and the README, and compared the diffs with the resolutions by reading them. No model output is used as legal text, and no model runs at data-build or git-build time.
